Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the motives framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 431. The resulting operator achieves bound O(229/n^4).

Theorem 220.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let the motives structure S satisfy the conditions of invariance principle framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 958. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 941 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 958 is immediate from the invariance principle property. For the inductive step, we decompose the motives structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 941. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
